KA JINGIM BAD JINGTREI U JOHN CALVIN 
I TJEN-LAM-KHMAT. 


Ka long kaba sngewbha sliibun ba I Rev. T. Rodborne 
1 la ioh ban lum ban pyrkhat bad la lah ban pynmih ia kane 
ka jingthoh (kitab) ba donkam shaphang ka jingim bad jing- 
trei u John Calvin. Ka dei ka jingthoh kaba donkam kyr- 
pang ia ki Khristan ban tip bad pyrkhat bha ha kine ki por 
ha kiba ka jingshliir bad jingngeit Khristan ba shisha ka 
duna eh hapdeng kiba bun. Ka don ka jingkhawpud ba 
kynja mynsiem (moral cowardice) ne ka jingbymnud ban 
ieng ha ka liang ka hok, ka jingbha bad ka jingshisha (Jong 
ka Ktien U Blei) wat hapdeng kito kiba dei ban long ki

nongialam ha ka Balang. 

Kumba la kdew u nongthoh, kane kan long kaba 
iarap ia kito kiba pule da pyrkhat, ban ioh jinghikai bad 
ioh mynsiem de na ka rukom im ka jingngeit bad ka 
jingtrei u John Calvin, kumno ban tei ia ka jingim bad jing* 
ngeit Khristan ha kine ki por kiba tynjuh ia ngi. 

U nongthoh u la buh ha ka rukom kaba lyngkot bad 
ba shai ruh shaphang ki Aiom ka jingpynshitom beift ia 
ka Balang, bad ha ka Balang hi naduh kaba sdang. U 
la kdew ruh ia kiba bun ki rukom jingiasaitnia bad jing- 
iapher jingmut kiba la mih hapdeng ki nongialam ka Balang. 



U la pyni ruh na kaba la jia la long ha bun ki jaka sha 
ri France, Switzerland, Germany bad ri Bilat de haduh 
katno ka jingkhwan bor bad jingsarong thala ki nongsynshar 
ia ka Balang bad ka Hima, kala pynlong ia ki kiba bishni, 
kiba runar bad kiba nud ban pyniap bein kumba pynhiar 
kput, ha ka rukom ba sngewshyrkhei ia kito ki riewngeit 
ki bym treh pdiang ia ki jingbuh bad jingdawa ki khlieh 
bad ki nongsynshar jong ka Balang Rome. 

La pynshai ba ki riewngeit ba shisha bad ba iaineh 
ki la hap shah pynshitom bad sliah pyniap kum ki martyr 
ha ki nongleh khrawbor bad kiba mane blcithaw jong ka 
Balang Rome bad jong ka pyrthei bymngeit, 

Ki la don ki bymnud ban ieng skhcm ha khmat ki jing-* 
pynshitom ba sngewshyrkhei; hynrci kiba bun na ki riew- 
ngeit ki la shah da ka jingkmen bad jingsngewshngaih ha 
la U Trai kum ki sakhi ia ka bor bad jingpynim ba phylla 
Jong U.

La ka kot kam da hch, u nongthoii u la buh lyngkot 
shaphang kiwei pat ki riewshlur Khristan jong ka jingpyn- 
thymmai niam (ba kynja mynsiom) kiba shuwa ia u John 
Calvin bad kiba ha ka por u John Calvin de, kiba la iaieh 
ban pynbha ne pyiibeit tliymmai ia ki jinghikai ba bakla 
ka Balang kiba pher na ki jinghikai ka Testament Ba- 
thymmai. 

Ka jinghun mynsiem bad j ngiaid shaphrang ba shisha 
ka shong ba kaba ngi bat skhem bad bud belt ia ki jinghikai 



bad jinglong ba bha kiba la paw ha ka jingim bad jingphla 
U Trai jong ngi U Jisu Khrist. 

Kumta kane ka kot kan iarap ban ai mynsietn bad 
jingsblur thynimai ban ieng na ka bynta U Khris, ka hok 
bad ka jingshisha jong U ha kine ki por ba la paw bun jait 
ki lad bad ki rukom jingstad ba phor ba pher. 

La buh thikna ia katto katne ki kam bad jingpyr- 
shang K:i ka jingim u John Calvin, kioa lah ban ai jingmut 
ia ki noiigpule ba pyrkhat shaphang ki bynta ba ngi dei 
ban pyrtliuli bud. La kdew shai dc ia ki rukom trei ba 
kordor jong uta u iiongseng ia ka Balang Presbyterian, 
uba ia pynicng bad pynphriang de ia ki jinghikai shong 
tynrai ka iram Khristan kat kum ka Ktien U BIci. 

Ki dar.g don shuh shuii kiei kici kiba mymtoi ba ngin 
tip Sii'iphang u John Calvin, hynrei kaba I Rev, T, 
Rodbornc 1 la buii bad la pynsJiai lyngkot ka la biang bha 
na k.i bynta kiba kham bun ki nongpule ha ka Balang 
jong ngi.

Lada pule ia kite da kaba sngewthuh ngiu ioh jingmut 
sjiibun ban pyrkhat tnymmai shaphang kumno ngin im 
ngin iaid bad aiu ngin Ich na ka bynta la U Trai bad U 
Nongpynim kum ki riewiigeit kiba la ioh mad ia ka bor 
bad jingjisnti ba khraw eh jong U. 

Ki don ki bynta ha kane hi ka kot kiba kham dei 
tang ia ki Minister bad ki nongialam ba pyrkhal ha ki 
Balang jong ngi. 



(iv) 

Kiba kwah ban da ioh jingtip kaba bniah shaphang 
ki kam, ki jingthoh bad jinghikai u John Calvin ki lah 
ban pule na ki kot ba la kdew u nongthoh ha ka jinglam- 
phrang, bad na lai saw tylli kiwei pat ki kot. 

Ngi don daw ban ainguh ia U Blei bad ban sngew- 
nguh ia u nongthoh na ka bynta kane ka kitab ba kordor 
ha ka ktien Khasi. 

Rr-v. Kelvinell. 

Principal 

Theological College, Cherrapunji. 



JINGLAMPHRANG

Ban sngewthuh shaphang ka jingim u briew, donkam 
ban tip katto katne, la kumno kumno, shaphang ka por 
ne ka aiom ha kaba uta u briew u im. Namarkata, la 
sngewdonkam ban iathuh lyngkot shaphang ki katto katne 
tylli ki jingjia ha ka history ka Balang shuwa ban poi sha 
kaba iathuh shaphang u John Calvin. Ki don artylli ki 
ktien kiba eh ban ai ktien Khasi, hynrei la lah hi ban 
sngewthuh namar ki la man kylla ktien Khasi. Kita ki 
long, (1) Reformation (kynnoh Riphormeshon), kaba mut 
ka jingpynthymmai niam; bad (2) Protestants^ kaba mut 
ki nongujor pyrshah. 

Lada iatainia bniah ki la don ki kynja Reformation 
bad ki Protestants naduh ka por ki Apostol hi. Kum ka 
nuksa ngi lah ban kdew ia ka jingujor pyrshah u Paul ia 
ka Balang Khristan jong ki Apostol hapoh ka jingialam u 
Petros haba ki hi hikai ba shuwa ban pdiang ia ki lentil 
sba ka Balang donkam ban da pynkylla Jiw ia ki. Ka 
history jong ka Balang ka dap da ki jingiathuh shaphang 
ki Reformation bad ki Protestation (Jingujor Pyrshah) ha 
ki jingiadei poh iing bad ki jingshah pynshitom beifi ha ki 
jingiadei bad ka pyrthei ba shabar; hynrei kim ju khot 
Reformation bad Protestation ia baroh ki jingialeh ba bun 
syrtap ban pynthymmai ia ka Balang haduh ka snem kum- 
ba 1500. Ki Iyer Reformation ki la don baroh shilynter 
ka history jong ka; hynrei kata ka Iyer bah kaba la khie

'^Ki ktiea khatdvb U Jisu Khrist ha la ki synran : “Khie

Ri India. Ki jong u Petros, u loannis bad u Paul ki ia trei 
ha ki jaka kiba jan ban pynskl.ein ia ka Balang. Kitei ki

ki la iarap ban pyniar stct ia ki pud ba ka Gospel ka la poi 
haduh ba ha kaba shiteug ka spah snem kaba saw ka la 
poi shaduh baroh ki shnong heh ka Asia Minor, ka Greece, 
Armenia, Persia, India, ka Ri Africa Mihngi, Sepngi bad 
Shatei, Spain, Italy, Gaul lane Ri France, Germany, 
Great Britain (Bilat) bad kiwei kiwei.
